Wednesday 10:37 PM


227 previous messages
What Was Wrong

The hydration path still had one GC landmine: PersistentWorldRegistry.TryResolveItemData() could fall back through record.ItemPersistentId.ToString() after a hash miss. That path is reachable during hydration apply, so it was not acceptable.

Unity also had stale compile blockers after domain reload: missing celestial registry compatibility and a stale Bee DAG reference. I fixed the real source gap and forced Unity to rebuild script inputs.

What I Did

Replaced managed persistent-id string fallback with fixed-buffer FNV hashing over FixedString128Bytes.
Added hydration performance telemetry: GlobalTelemetryBus.PublishPerformanceWarning when hydration apply exceeds 0.2ms.
Added registry-backed HectonCelestialEngine.ActiveRuntimeInstance, no local singleton ownership.
Reset GlobalRegistry celestial slot on subsystem registration.
Updated persistence smoke checks for the GC purge and telemetry hook.
Time-sliced hydration loop:

private async Awaitable RunHydrationSessionAsync(int sessionVersion)
{
    while (Application.isPlaying &&
           ReferenceEquals(GlobalRegistry.PersistentWorldRegistry, this) &&
           _hydrationSessionVersion == sessionVersion)
    {
        if (!TryProcessHydrationBurst())
            break;

        await Awaitable.NextFrameAsync(cancellationToken: destroyCancellationToken);
    }
}

private bool TryProcessHydrationBurst()
{
    long burstStartTicks = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
    long budgetDeadline = burstStartTicks + HydrationFrameBudgetTicks; // Stopwatch.Frequency / 250
    int processedCount = 0;

    while (processedCount < MaxHydrationsPerFrame &&
           _pendingHydrationReadIndex < _pendingHydrationRecords.Length)
    {
        if (processedCount > 0 && Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() >= budgetDeadline)
            break;

        ApplyOneHydrationRecord(_pendingHydrationRecords[_pendingHydrationReadIndex++]);
        processedCount++;
    }

    if (processedCount > 0)
        PublishHydrationBudgetWarningIfNeeded(Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() - burstStartTicks);

    return _pendingHydrationReadIndex < _pendingHydrationRecords.Length;
}
AAA Cheat

Deleted the honest managed-string identity fallback. Replacement: hash the fixed persistent-id bytes directly and use hash lookup only. It is cheaper, deterministic, and avoids hydration-path garbage.

Hydration Loop

long burstStartTicks = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
long budgetDeadline = burstStartTicks + HydrationFrameBudgetTicks;
int processedCount = 0;
while (processedCount < MaxHydrationsPerFrame &&
       _pendingHydrationReadIndex < _pendingHydrationRecords.Length)
{
    if (processedCount > 0 && Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() >= budgetDeadline)
        break;

    int recordIndex = _pendingHydrationRecords[_pendingHydrationReadIndex++];
    processedCount++;

    if (!IsValidRecordIndex(recordIndex))
        continue;

    PersistentWorldItemRecord record = _records[recordIndex];
    ClearHydrationQueuedFlag(in record);
    if (record.IsCollected || IsDeletedInstanceUid(record.InstanceUid))
        continue;

    HydrateRecord(recordIndex, in record);
}

Hydration Loop

private async Awaitable RunHydrationSessionAsync(int sessionVersion)
{
    while (_hydrationSessionActive && sessionVersion == _hydrationSessionVersion)
    {
        if (!TryProcessHydrationBurst())
            break;

        await Awaitable.NextFrameAsync(cancellationToken: destroyCancellationToken);
    }
}

private bool TryProcessHydrationBurst()
{
    long burstStartTicks = Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();
    long budgetDeadline = burstStartTicks + HydrationFrameBudgetTicks;

    while (processedCount < MaxHydrationsPerFrame &&
           _pendingHydrationReadIndex < _pendingHydrationRecords.Length)
    {
        // apply one hydration record

        if (Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() >= budgetDeadline)
        {
            PublishHydrationBudgetWarningIfNeeded(Stopwatch.GetTimestamp() - burstStartTicks);
            return true;
        }
    }

    return _pendingHydrationReadIndex < _pendingHydrationRecords.Length;
}

[0. SITREP: THE VR PERFORMANCE TRAP]
VR on an MX350/Quest 2 is a suicide mission unless we use Single Pass Instanced (SPI) rendering. You must ensure our AUP (64-bit coordinates) and shader cheats work for both eyes without ghosting or jitter.

[I. PRIMARY OBJECTIVES: XR ARCHITECTURE]
1. SINGLE PASS INSTANCED (SPI) AUDIT: Force all shaders (CoreLit, Voxel, UI) to support UNITY_VERTEX_INPUT_INSTANCE_ID and UNITY_SETUP_INSTANCE_ID. Any shader that draws only in one eye is a CRITICAL FAILURE.
2. AUP XR REBASE: When HectonFloatingOrigin shifts, you must ensure both Eye Matrices are recalculated atomically. If there is a 1-frame delta between eyes, the player will suffer instant motion sickness.
3. FOVEATED RENDERING (QUALITY FAKE): Implement a Variable Rate Shading (VRS) simulator. In the fragment shader, use a radial mask to reduce texture sampling quality or skip expensive ALU math for pixels > 30 degrees from the lens center.
4. XR INPUT DISPATCHER: Map the GlobalRegistry.NativeInputManager to the OpenXR controller profile. Capture Trigger/Grip/Joystick data into a NativeArray<XRInputState> for the Burst systems to consume.
5. NEAR-CLIP PLANE DITHER: VR players stick their heads into walls. When the visor AUP is < 0.1m from a Voxel wall, trigger an aggressive Blue Noise Dither to fade the wall out, preventing geometry clipping through the brain.
6. VRAM VR REAPER: VR textures (Eye Buffers) eat 40% more VRAM. Adjust VRAMPressureMonitor to trigger Mip-Map dropping at 1.2GB instead of 1.4GB when in VR mode.
7. 72Hz CADENCE LOCK: Sync the SystemDispatcher to the XR Refresh Rate. If the Quest 2 is at 72Hz, our FastTick must align perfectly to 13.8ms to prevent micro-stuttering.
8. ZERO-GC EYE TRACKING STUB: Even if the hardware doesn't support it, create a NativeArray<RaycastCommand> for "Look-at" selection that follows the camera forward vector, enabling O(1) eye-selection for menus.

[II. THE "EXPENSIVE LIE" ENFORCEMENT]
- Replace 3D volumetric "Light Shafts" in VR with a 2D screen-space radial blur that is calculated ONCE for both eyes to save 50% GPU cost.

[III. EVIDENCE-BASED REPORTING]
1. SURGERY LOG: Provide the HLSL code for the SPI support in Hecton_CoreLit.
2. DIFFS: Provide complete Git Diffs.

MASTER PROMPT: PROJECT "HECTON-8" — MEGADOMAIN: SYSTEM ORCHESTRATION
AUTHORITY: CTO / Lead Architect
OPERATIONAL MODE: OMEGA PROTOCOL / LATENCY & STUTTER PURGE

[0. SITREP: THE AWAITABLE MASK]
The 3.0s dive transition looks cinematic, but using Awaitable.NextFrameAsync() to spread pool initialization is creating massive input latency when the game actually starts. We are masking stutters by creating unresponsiveness.

[I. OMEGA-LEVEL ARCHITECTURE (DEEP SURGERY)]
1. TIME-BUDGETED BOOTSTRAP: Replace the "50 instances per frame" rule. Use a hard Stopwatch budget. Instantiate objects until ElapsedMilliseconds > 8.0ms, then yield. This ensures 60fps consistency regardless of hardware speed.
2. ASYNC SCENE ACTIVATION LOCK: Ensure allowSceneActivation = true is ONLY called exactly at t = 3.0s of the dissolve shader. Do not activate the scene early, as Unity's internal Awake calls will stutter the dissolve animation.
3. QUADRATIC INPUT DEADZONE (NO BRANCHING): The (v * v) input curve must not use if (magnitude < deadzone) return 0;. Use math.max(0, magnitude - deadzone) to avoid CPU branch misprediction penalties.
4. DOF BLEND TWEENING: The 0.2s Depth of Field animation must NOT use a coroutine. Drive the DoF focal length in SystemDispatcher.LateUpdate using a deterministic math.lerp based on Time.unscaledTime.
5. FREEZE FRAME DITHER CULLING: When Time.timeScale == 0 (Pause), disable all animations, particle systems, and ocean fluid displacement shaders globally via a _GamePaused uniform to drop GPU load to absolute minimum.
6. AMBIENT SHEDDING ENFORCEMENT: The 5-Artery Mega-Bus must strictly enforce the 2.0ms budget. If EnvironmentEvents are dropped, log a 1 to an internal DroppedEventsCounter for Black Box telemetry, without allocating a string.
7. TERMINAL BOOT CHAR SPAN: Ensure the "LOADING NEURAL INTERFACE..." text uses a static readonly byte[] converted to Span<char> via UnsafeUtility, avoiding any C# string literal allocation in the boot sequence.
8. PLAYMODE SENTINEL FILE I/O: The CodexPlayModeLauncher .flag file must be written using asynchronous file streams (FileStream.WriteAsync) so it doesn't cause a micro-stutter in the Editor.

[II. THE "EXPENSIVE LIE" ENFORCEMENT]
- The "Low-frequency procedural camera heave" during the dive must be a mathematical sine wave applied to the Camera's local Y-axis in LateUpdate. Do NOT use Unity's Animator or AnimationCurves.

AAA Cheat
Physical center-eye DoF raycast remains disabled by CenterEyeFocusRaycastEnabled = false; ray buffers do not allocate unless the gate is deliberately enabled. This keeps the focus effect as a cinematic approximation instead of a per-frame physics query.

Seam Exact Lock

[BurstCompile(FloatPrecision.Standard, FloatMode.Deterministic)]
public struct SnapDualSDFTopCellsToTerrainJob : IJobParallelFor
{
    [NativeDisableParallelForRestriction] public NativeArray<float> Sdf;
    [NativeDisableParallelForRestriction] public NativeArray<float> SecondarySdf;

    public void Execute(int index)
    {
        int z = index / SdfWidth;
        int x = index - z * SdfWidth;
        float terrainHeight = SampleTerrainHeight(
            SdfOriginAup.x + x * (double)VoxelSizeMeters,
            SdfOriginAup.z + z * (double)VoxelSizeMeters);

        float terrainY = (terrainHeight - (float)SdfOriginAup.y) / VoxelSizeMeters;
        int lowerY = (int)math.floor(terrainY);
        WriteExactTerrainDensity(x, lowerY, z, terrainHeight);
        WriteExactTerrainDensity(x, lowerY + 1, z, terrainHeight);
    }

    private void WriteExactTerrainDensity(int x, int y, int z, float terrainHeight)
    {
        if (y < 0 || y >= SdfHeight) return;
        int i = x + y * SdfWidth + z * SdfWidth * SdfHeight;
        float density = terrainHeight - (float)(SdfOriginAup.y + y * (double)VoxelSizeMeters);
        Sdf[i] = density;
        SecondarySdf[i] = density;
    }
}

Seam Code
Core Burst seam lock is in 
HectonAnomalySdfJobs.cs (line 223)
:

[BurstCompile(FloatPrecision.Standard, FloatMode.Deterministic)]
public struct SnapDualSDFTopCellsToTerrainJob : IJobParallelFor
{
    [ReadOnly] public NativeArray<float> TerrainHeights;
    [NativeDisableParallelForRestriction] public NativeArray<float> Sdf;
    [NativeDisableParallelForRestriction] public NativeArray<float> SecondarySdf;
    public byte WriteSecondary;

    public void Execute(int index)
    {
        int z = index / SdfWidth;
        int x = index - z * SdfWidth;
        double absX = SdfOriginAup.x + x * (double)VoxelSizeMeters;
        double absZ = SdfOriginAup.z + z * (double)VoxelSizeMeters;
        float terrainHeight = SampleTerrainHeight(absX, absZ);
        float terrainY = (terrainHeight - (float)SdfOriginAup.y) / VoxelSizeMeters;
        int lowerY = (int)math.trunc(terrainY);

        WriteExactTerrainDensity(x, lowerY, z, terrainHeight);
        WriteExactTerrainDensity(x, lowerY + 1, z, terrainHeight);
    }

    private void WriteExactTerrainDensity(int x, int y, int z, float terrainHeight)
    {
        int index = x + y * SdfWidth + z * SdfWidth * SdfHeight;
        float absY = (float)(SdfOriginAup.y + y * (double)VoxelSizeMeters);
        float density = terrainHeight - absY;
        Sdf[index] = density;
        if (WriteSecondary != 0 && SecondarySdf.IsCreated && (uint)index < (uint)SecondarySdf.Length)
            SecondarySdf[index] = density;
    }
}
Production sets WriteSecondary = 0 at 
HectonVoxelEngine.cs (line 4666)
, so the seam writes one authoritative SDF field.

SEAM CODE

public struct SnapDualSDFTopCellsToTerrainJob : Unity.Jobs.IJobParallelFor
{
    [NativeDisableParallelForRestriction] public NativeArray<float> Sdf;
    [NativeDisableParallelForRestriction] public NativeArray<float> SecondarySdf;
    public byte WriteSecondary;

    public void Execute(int index)
    {
        int z = index / SdfWidth;
        int x = index - z * SdfWidth;
        float terrainHeight = SampleTerrainHeight(absX, absZ);
        int lowerY = (int)math.trunc((terrainHeight - (float)SdfOriginAup.y) / VoxelSizeMeters);

        WriteExactTerrainDensity(x, lowerY, z, terrainHeight);
        WriteExactTerrainDensity(x, lowerY + 1, z, terrainHeight);
    }
}
Full implementation: 
HectonAnomalySdfJobs.cs (line 223)
. Production disables secondary writes via WriteSecondary = 0 at 
HectonVoxelEngine.cs (line 4776)
.

Visor Crack HLSL

void ComputePressureCrackParallax(float2 uv, float intensity, float timeValue, out float crackMask, out float2 parallaxOffset)
{
    float active = saturate(intensity);
    crackMask = ComputePressureLensCrackMask(uv, active, timeValue);

    float sampleStep = 0.0018;
    float crackDx =
        ComputePressureLensCrackMask(uv + float2(sampleStep, 0.0), active, timeValue) -
        ComputePressureLensCrackMask(uv - float2(sampleStep, 0.0), active, timeValue);
    float crackDy =
        ComputePressureLensCrackMask(uv + float2(0.0, sampleStep), active, timeValue) -
        ComputePressureLensCrackMask(uv - float2(0.0, sampleStep), active, timeValue);

    float2 crackGradient = float2(crackDx, crackDy);
    float gradientMagnitude = saturate(length(crackGradient) * 12.0);
    float2 crackNormal = normalize(crackGradient + float2(0.0001, 0.0001));
    float2 eyeParallax = (uv - 0.5) * (0.45 + gradientMagnitude);
    float shardDepth = crackMask * active * _PressureCrackParallaxDepth;
    parallaxOffset = (crackNormal * _PressureCrackNormalStrength * gradientMagnitude + eyeParallax) * shardDepth;
}

FNV-1a Kernel
Current Burst-addressable quest hash kernel is in 
QuestRuntimeTypes.cs (line 166)
:

[BurstCompile]
internal static unsafe class QuestFlagHashKernel
{
    public static uint ComputeStableHash(ReadOnlySpan<char> value)
    {
        if (value.Length <= 0)
            return 0u;

        fixed (char* valuePtr = value)
            return ComputeStableHash((ushort*)valuePtr, value.Length);
    }

    [BurstCompile]
    public static uint ComputeStableHash(ushort* value, int length)
    {
        if (value == null || length <= 0)
            return 0u;

        unchecked
        {
            uint hash = Hecton.Localization.LocHash.FnvOffsetBasis;
            for (int i = 0; i < length; i++)
            {
                ushort current = value[i];
                hash ^= (byte)current;
                hash *= Hecton.Localization.LocHash.FnvPrime;
                hash ^= (byte)(current >> 8);
                hash *= Hecton.Localization.LocHash.FnvPrime;
            }

            return hash;
        }
    }
}

Core breaker code:

internal static bool TryConsumeBaseStressCascadeEvent(int islandId, uint eventHash)
{
    int currentFrame = Time.frameCount;
    if (_baseStressCascadeBreakerFrame != currentFrame)
        ResetBaseStressCascadeCircuitBreakerStateForFrame(currentFrame);

    int safeIslandId = math.max(0, islandId);
    int slot = ResolveBaseStressCascadeSlot(safeIslandId);
    if (slot < 0)
    {
        CrashTelemetryBuffer.ReportEventCascadeWarning();
        GlobalTelemetryBus.PublishCatastrophicCascadePrevented(unchecked((uint)safeIslandId), eventHash, 1);
        return false;
    }

    ushort currentCount = _baseStressCascadeIslandCounts[slot];
    if (currentCount < MaxBaseStressCascadeEventsPerIslandPerFrame)
    {
        _baseStressCascadeIslandCounts[slot] = (ushort)(currentCount + 1);
        return true;
    }

    _baseStressCascadeDroppedCounts[slot]++;
    if (!_baseStressCascadeTelemetryEmitted[slot])
    {
        _baseStressCascadeTelemetryEmitted[slot] = true;
        CrashTelemetryBuffer.ReportEventCascadeWarning();
        GlobalTelemetryBus.PublishCatastrophicCascadePrevented(
            unchecked((uint)safeIslandId), eventHash, _baseStressCascadeDroppedCounts[slot]);
    }

    return false;
}

<system_prompt>
MASTER PROMPT: PROJECT "HECTON-8" — MEGADOMAIN: STREAMING & ASSET MEMORY
AUTHORITY: CTO / Lead Architect
OPERATIONAL MODE: THE INQUISITOR OF VRAM
THINKING LEVEL: MAXIMUM (Addressables, Zero-GC, Awaitable, ColdTick)[0. SITREP: THE MEMORY LEAK TERROR]
If we don't aggressively unload memory, the MX350 will crash in 10 minutes. You will build the ruthless memory governor.

[I. MANDATORY RECONNAISSANCE]
1. Inspect AssetLifecycleGovernor.cs and VRAMPressureMonitor.cs.

[II. PRIMARY OBJECTIVES: MEMORY DICTATORSHIP]
1. ADDRESSABLES REF-COUNT PURGE: Ensure AssetLifecycleGovernor forcibly calls Addressables.Release on any chunk asset that is > 1500m away from the Player AUP in ColdTick (1Hz).
2. AWAITABLE CHUNK STREAMING: Chunk loading must use await Awaitable.NextFrameAsync(). Implement a hard budget: if chunk gen logic takes > 2.0ms in a frame, force a yield.
3. VRAM MIP-MAP DROPPING: In VRAMPressureMonitor, if VRAM > 1.6GB, forcibly lower QualitySettings.globalTextureMipmapLimit to 1 (Half Res). Restore to 0 below 1.2GB.
4. COLD-TICK UNLOADING: Move all chunk-distance checks and release logic to ColdTick. Do not check distances every frame.

Mesh Pool Code

private const int VoxelSurfaceMeshPoolSize = 256;

private static readonly Mesh[] _voxelSurfaceMeshPool = new Mesh[VoxelSurfaceMeshPoolSize];
private static readonly bool[] _voxelSurfaceMeshPoolInUse = new bool[VoxelSurfaceMeshPoolSize];

private static void EnsureVoxelSurfaceMeshPool()
{
    for (int i = 0; i < VoxelSurfaceMeshPoolSize; i++)
    {
        if (_voxelSurfaceMeshPool[i] != null)
            continue;

        Mesh mesh = new Mesh();
        mesh.name = "HectonVoxelSurface_Pooled";
        mesh.indexFormat = IndexFormat.UInt32;
        mesh.MarkDynamic();
        _voxelSurfaceMeshPool[i] = mesh;
    }
}

internal static Mesh AcquireVoxelSurfaceMesh()
{
    EnsureVoxelSurfaceMeshPool();

    for (int i = 0; i < VoxelSurfaceMeshPoolSize; i++)
    {
        if (_voxelSurfaceMeshPoolInUse[i])
            continue;

        Mesh mesh = _voxelSurfaceMeshPool[i];
        _voxelSurfaceMeshPoolInUse[i] = true;
        mesh.Clear(false);
        return mesh;
    }

    return null;
}

internal static bool ReleaseVoxelSurfaceMesh(Mesh mesh)
{
    for (int i = 0; i < VoxelSurfaceMeshPoolSize; i++)
    {
        if (!ReferenceEquals(_voxelSurfaceMeshPool[i], mesh))
            continue;

        mesh.Clear(false);
        _voxelSurfaceMeshPoolInUse[i] = false;
        return true;
    }

    return false;
}

AAA Cheat
The density path probe now uses dominant-axis stepping:

Vector3 pathDelta = endWorldPosition - startWorldPosition;
float dominantAxisLength = Mathf.Max(
    Mathf.Abs(pathDelta.x),
    Mathf.Max(Mathf.Abs(pathDelta.y), Mathf.Abs(pathDelta.z)));
int sampleCount = Mathf.Max(2, Mathf.CeilToInt(dominantAxisLength / sampleStep));
Vector3 sampleDelta = pathDelta / sampleCount;
This is a cinematic approximation. It avoids Euclidean sqrt and per-sample lerp. It may sample less densely on diagonals; that is the performance trade.

6. MASS LOCOMOTION CACHE: The TotalMassKg must NOT be recalculated every frame. The PlayerInventory recalculates mass ONLY when an item is added or removed, caches the maxSwimSpeed multiplier, and the KCC reads the cached float.
7. LASER CUTTER HEAT SHADER LINK: The thermal glow of the rock being cut must be driven by passing the Laser's CurrentHeat directly to the _LaserHitHeat global shader uniform, avoiding any CPU material property block instancing per-frame.
8. RADIX SORT JOB ALLOCATION: The InventoryRadixSortJob MUST use Allocator.TempJob and be immediately disposed after the sort completes. Do not leave NativeArrays lingering after the user closes the inventory.[II. THE "EXPENSIVE LIE" ENFORCEMENT]

Inventory Mass Cache

private void NotifyInventoryChanged(bool markDirty = true, bool massDirty = true)
{
    if (markDirty)
    {
        MarkInventoryDirty();
        RefreshInventoryShadowBufferFromRuntime();
    }

    if (massDirty)
        MarkMassCacheDirty();

    if (_massCacheDirty)
        RefreshDerivedMassAndSurvivalLoad();

    PublishEncumbranceChanged();
    InventoryVersion++;
    InventoryEvents.NotifyInventoryChanged();
    InventoryChanged?.Invoke();
}

private void RefreshDerivedMassAndSurvivalLoad()
{
    if (_grid == null ||
        !_stackCounts.IsCreated ||
        !_anchorUnitMassKg.IsCreated ||
        !_anchorUnitVolumeM3.IsCreated ||
        !_anchorUnitRadiationSv.IsCreated ||
        !_derivedMassVolumeScratch.IsCreated)
    {
        ApplyDerivedMassTotals(float3.zero);
    }
    else
    {
        new InventoryMassVolumeJob
        {
            AnchorHashIds = _grid.AnchorHashIds,
            StackCounts = _stackCounts,
            AnchorUnitMassKg = _anchorUnitMassKg,
            AnchorUnitVolumeM3 = _anchorUnitVolumeM3,
            AnchorUnitRadiationSv = _anchorUnitRadiationSv,
            Totals = _derivedMassVolumeScratch
        }.Execute();

        ApplyDerivedMassTotals(_derivedMassVolumeScratch[0]);
    }

    _massCacheDirty = false;
}

private void ApplyDerivedMassTotals(float3 totals)
{
    TotalMassKg = math.max(0f, totals.x);
    TotalVolumeM3 = math.max(0f, totals.y);
    TotalRadiationSv = math.max(0f, totals.z);

    float carryCapacityKg = ResolveCarryCapacityKilograms();
    CachedInventoryLoad01 = math.saturate(TotalMassKg / carryCapacityKg);
    CachedMaxSwimSpeedMultiplier = math.lerp(1f, InventoryLoadMinimumMovementMultiplier, CachedInventoryLoad01);

    HectonPlayerMovement movement = TryResolveMovementLoadSink();
    if (movement != null)
        movement.ApplyRuntimeInventoryMassLoad(TotalMassKg, carryCapacityKg, CachedMaxSwimSpeedMultiplier, CachedInventoryLoad01);
}

7. 1:1 COCKPIT ROTATION: When inside the submarine, the cockpit mesh must be perfectly parented to the VR tracking space. The sub's rotation must apply to the *parent* of the VR rig, not the camera.
8. VERTICALITY STABILIZER: Implement a "Horizon Lock" option. When swimming vertically in the abyss, keep the player's roll axis at zero unless they manually tilt the joystick.

8. AURORA NOISE TEXTURE REUSE: The scrolling UV noise for the Aurora Borealis MUST reuse the exact same noise texture loaded into memory for the Water Caustics. Do not load a separate 2D noise map.[II. THE "EXPENSIVE LIE" ENFORCEMENT]

[0. SITREP: GHOST HANDS ARE BANNED]
In HECTON-8, you are not a floating camera. You are an engineer in a heavy suit. Your hands must have physical presence, weight, and collision.

[I. PRIMARY OBJECTIVES: PHYSICAL INTERACTION]
1. CONTEXTUAL HAND IK (FABRIK): Implement a Burst-compiled FABRIK (Forward And Backward Reaching Inverse Kinematics) system for the player's arms. Hands must latch onto IKinematicRepairTarget points with AUP precision.
2. PHYSICS-BASED GRABBING: Do not parent objects to hands. Use a "Soft-Lock" velocity drive. When a player grabs a 50kg titanium plate, the hand must lag behind the controller slightly to simulate weight.
3. SUIT COLLISION GEOMETRY: The hands MUST be part of the CollisionDetectionMode.ContinuousSpeculative logic. If a hand is crushed by a bulkhead, trigger a SuitDamageEvent.
4. HAPTIC RUMBLE ROUTING: Connect the PhysicsEventBus directly to controller haptics. Scraping a wall with the left hand must trigger a high-frequency micro-rumble in the left Touch controller.
5. TOOL ATTACHMENT OFFSETS: Every industrial tool (Scanner, Cutter) must have a unique NativeArray<float4x4> for VR hand-grip offsets. No generic "gun" grip. 
6. LEVER & SWITCH INTERACTION: Interacting with cockpit switches must use a "Snap-to-Angle" logic. Once a finger is near a toggle, it mathematically snaps, and the controller rumble provides a "Click" feel.
7. VESTIGIAL ARM CULLING: Do not render the upper arms if the camera FOV is narrow. Only render what is visible to prevent "elbow-clipping" horror in VR.
8. TWO-HANDED MASS SCALE: If an object is >20kg, require both hands to stabilize its velocity. If one hand is released, apply a rotational torque to simulate the object falling.

[II. THE "EXPENSIVE LIE" ENFORCEMENT]
- Use a 1D Look-Up Texture (LUT) to modulate haptic intensity based on the player's current depth and suit-integrity, making everything feel "vague" and "numb" at high pressure.

Continuous Speculative Switch

private void ArmSafeTeleportSpeculativeCcd(Rigidbody body)
{
    RegisterTrackedBodyInternal(body);
    int bodyIndex = FindTrackedBodyIndex(body);
    RigidbodyState bodyState = _bodyStates[bodyIndex];

    bodyState.SafeTeleportSpeculativeCcdActive = true;
    bodyState.SafeTeleportSpeculativeFixedTicksRemaining = SafeTeleportSpeculativeFixedTickHold;
    body.collisionDetectionMode = CollisionDetectionMode.ContinuousSpeculative;

    _bodyStates[bodyIndex] = bodyState;
}

private void TickSafeTeleportSpeculativeCcdGuards()
{
    // after one PostFixed tick:
    body.collisionDetectionMode = CollisionDetectionMode.Discrete;
}

AUP rebase Burst job:

[BurstCompile(FloatMode = FloatMode.Fast, FloatPrecision = FloatPrecision.Standard)]
private struct WreckMatrixRebaseJob : IJobParallelFor
{
    public NativeArray<Matrix4x4> Matrices;
    public float3 RuntimeOffset;

    public void Execute(int index)
    {
        Matrix4x4 matrix = Matrices[index];
        matrix.m03 += RuntimeOffset.x;
        matrix.m13 += RuntimeOffset.y;
        matrix.m23 += RuntimeOffset.z;
        Matrices[index] = matrix;
    }
}

Core cull job is in 
WreckMaterialRegistry.cs (line 48)
:

[BurstCompile(FloatMode = FloatMode.Fast, FloatPrecision = FloatPrecision.Standard)]
private struct CullWreckMatricesToVisibleSubsetJob : IJob
{
    [ReadOnly] public NativeArray<Matrix4x4> SourceMatrices;
    [ReadOnly] public NativeArray<float> SourceAges;
    [ReadOnly] public NativeArray<float4> FrustumPlanes;
    public NativeList<Matrix4x4> VisibleMatrices;
    public NativeList<float> VisibleAges;

    public void Execute()
    {
        VisibleMatrices.Clear();
        VisibleAges.Clear();

        for (int index = 0; index < SourceCount; index++)
        {
            Matrix4x4 matrix = SourceMatrices[index];
            if (EnableFrustumCulling && !IsMatrixAabbVisible(in matrix))
                continue;

            VisibleMatrices.AddNoResize(matrix);
            VisibleAges.AddNoResize(math.saturate(HasAgeData ? SourceAges[index] : 0.5f));
        }
    }
}

[0. SITREP: NO MORE MAGIC BUILDING]
Base building in HECTON-8 VR is a manual, industrial task. You don't just "place" a room; you weld it and wire it.

[I. PRIMARY OBJECTIVES: TACTILE CONSTRUCTION]
1. PHYSICAL WELDING PIPELINE: To build a wall, the player must hold the "Thermal Lance" tool to 4 AUP corner points. Implement a "Welding Progress" state per vertex.
2. CABLE DRAGGING (VR): To connect power, the player must grab a physical cable plug from a socket and manually walk it to the next module. Use a simplified spline math (4 points) to render the cable.
3. VALVE & HATCH OPERATION: Airlocks must require a physical 360-degree rotation of the wheel handle. Track the controller's angular delta and map it to the hatch's isOpen01 state.
4. TOOL MAINTENANCE (VR): Tools have physical parts. Changing a battery in a Scanner requires physically opening the battery door, pulling the old cell, and inserting the new one.
5. MODULAR HULL LOGIC: Instead of pre-baked rooms, use a "Support Frame" system. Players place beams, then weld panels onto them. Each panel is an entry in the LogisticsNetworkGraph.
6. TACTILE FABRICATOR: The Fabricator has a physical lever. To start a craft, the player must pull the lever. To cancel, they must hit the physical emergency stop button.
7. LEAK PLUGGING: If a base module is breached, the player must grab a "Repair Patch" and physically hold it against the spray while the Lance welds it. Use the AcousticImpulse to guide the player to the leak.
8. ZERO-GC PIPE PLACEMENT: Ensure that previewing pipe paths in VR uses Graphics.DrawMeshInstanced for the "holographic blueprint" look. No spawning GameObjects for previews.
